#METABOLOMICS WORKBENCH zys1986062186_20240718_003241 DATATRACK_ID:5021 STUDY_ID:ST003390 ANALYSIS_ID:AN005559 PROJECT_ID:PR002101
VERSION                          	1
CREATED_ON                       	10-09-2024
#PROJECT
PR:PROJECT_TITLE                 	In-depth profiling of biosignatures for Type 2 diabetes mellitus cohort
PR:PROJECT_TITLE                 	utilizing an integrated targeted LC-MS platform
PR:PROJECT_TYPE                  	Observational study
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	We proposed an optimized and in-depth target-based metabolome platform through
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	an integration of six distinct conditions, including a normal phase principle, a
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	pre-column chemical derivatization, as well as four reversed phase separation
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	methods for the absolute quantification and profiling of a total of 1609 small
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	molecules (32 sub-classes) in serum after normalization using isotope labeled
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	internal standards. Herein, we present a new dataset of a metabolomic profiles
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	encompassing a cohort of 200 healthy individuals and 100 newly diagnosed Type 2
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	diabetes mellitus patients from the northern region of China. We hereby make
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	these technical validation results and the profiling dataset publicly available
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	to the scientific community, showcasing its exceptional sensitivity and
PR:PROJECT_SUMMARY               	robustness as an invaluable tool for metabolome analysis across laboratories.
PR:INSTITUTE                     	The First Affiliated Hospital of Dalian Medical University
PR:LABORATORY                    	Laboratory of Integrative Medicine
PR:LAST_NAME                     	Ma
PR:FIRST_NAME                    	Shurong
PR:ADDRESS                       	No.222 Zhongshan Road, Xigang District, Dalian City, Liaoning Province, Dalian,
PR:ADDRESS                       	Xigang District, Dalian City, Liaoning Province, 116000, China
PR:EMAIL                         	mashurong@dmu.edu.cn
PR:PHONE                         	+86-411-93635863
PR:DOI                           	http://dx.doi.org/10.21228/M81V7G
#STUDY
ST:STUDY_TITLE                   	In-depth profiling of biosignatures for Type 2 diabetes mellitus cohort
ST:STUDY_TITLE                   	utilizing an integrated targeted LC-MS platform
ST:STUDY_TYPE                    	Observational study
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	A cohort of 200 healthy individuals and 100 newly diagnosed Type 2 diabetes
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	mellitus patients from the northern region of China were conducted to profiling
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	the metabolic abnormalities in the condition of diabetes.Then, the data by
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	methods M1-M6 was merged and uploaded as a whole file. The overall differential
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	analysis results were summarized, indicating an obvious metabolic disturbance in
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	amino acid, fatty acids, lysophosphatidyl choline (LysoPC), and triacylglycerol
ST:STUDY_SUMMARY                 	(TAG) under T2DM.
ST:INSTITUTE                     	The First Affiliated Hospital of Dalian Medical University
ST:LABORATORY                    	Laboratory of Integrative Medicine
ST:LAST_NAME                     	Ma
ST:FIRST_NAME                    	Shurong
ST:ADDRESS                       	No.222 Zhongshan Road, Xigang District, Dalian City, Liaoning Province
ST:EMAIL                         	mashurong@dmu.edu.cn
ST:PHONE                         	+86-411-83635863
ST:SUBMIT_DATE                   	2024-07-18
#SUBJECT
SU:SUBJECT_TYPE                  	Human
SU:SUBJECT_SPECIES               	Homo sapiens
SU:TAXONOMY_ID                   	9606
SU:AGE_OR_AGE_RANGE              	20-74
SU:WEIGHT_OR_WEIGHT_RANGE        	41.1-128.9kg
SU:HEIGHT_OR_HEIGHT_RANGE        	145-196.5cm
SU:GENDER                        	Male and female
SU:HUMAN_ETHNICITY               	Yellow race
SU:HUMAN_TRIAL_TYPE              	No-intervention
SU:HUMAN_LIFESTYLE_FACTORS       	N/A
SU:HUMAN_MEDICATIONS             	No-medication
SU:HUMAN_PRESCRIPTION_OTC        	No-prescription
SU:HUMAN_SMOKING_STATUS          	N/A
SU:HUMAN_ALCOHOL_DRUG_USE        	N/A
SU:HUMAN_NUTRITION               	Gneral nutrition
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	Inclusion criteria for T2DM patients: (1) they were initially diagnosed with
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	diabetes without known history of this disease, (2) they perform with no prior
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	history of diabetes medication for more than three months. The criteria for the
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	healthy control group were: (1) age between 20 and 70 years, (2) they have not
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	been diagnosed with acute or chronic cardiovascular and cerebrovascular
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	diseases; (3) they have not been diagnosed with tumors, (4) they agree to
SU:HUMAN_INCLUSION_CRITERIA      	participate in the study.
SU:HUMAN_EXCLUSION_CRITERIA      	Exclusion criteria: (1) they have been previously diagnosed with metabolic
SU:HUMAN_EXCLUSION_CRITERIA      	syndrome and hyperlipidemia; (2) prior or combined with tumor; (3) they could
SU:HUMAN_EXCLUSION_CRITERIA      	not tolerate blood collection for severe hemorrhagic disease; (4) onset of other
SU:HUMAN_EXCLUSION_CRITERIA      	diseases such as cardiovascular or cerebrovascular diseases; (5) they refuse to
SU:HUMAN_EXCLUSION_CRITERIA      	participate in the study or request the withdrawal of informed consent.

#COLLECTION
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	We totally recruited 200 individuals as healthy controls, and 100 patients of
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	newly diagnosed Type 2 diabetes mellitus (T2DM). All the subjects were enrolled
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	at the physical examination center and Department of Endocrinology and
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	Metabolism in The First Affiliated Hospital of Dalian Medical University from
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	July 2022 to November 2022. The primary T2DM patients were pathologically
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	diagnosed by standards of medical care in diabetes revised by The American
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	Diabetes Association (2021 edition). The protocol has received approval from the
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	institutional research ethics committee of The First Affiliated Hospital of
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	Dalian Medical University (approval no. PJ-KS-KY-2022-325), and written informed
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	consents were obtained from all the participants. For each subject, the general
CO:COLLECTION_SUMMARY            	medical and biochemical examination parameter were recorded.
CO:SAMPLE_TYPE                   	Blood (serum)
CO:COLLECTION_METHOD             	Fasting blood samples were collected in the additive-free sterile glass and
CO:COLLECTION_METHOD             	stand at room temprature for 1h. Then, serum samples were obtained by
CO:COLLECTION_METHOD             	centrifugation at 3500rpm under 4 â for 15min and dispensed into ep tubes,
CO:COLLECTION_METHOD             	storing at -80 â until analysis.
CO:COLLECTION_LOCATION           	The First Affiliated Hospital of Dalian Medical University
CO:COLLECTION_FREQUENCY          	Once
CO:COLLECTION_DURATION           	July 2022 to November 2022
CO:VOLUMEORAMOUNT_COLLECTED      	1ml
CO:STORAGE_CONDITIONS            	-80â
#TREATMENT
TR:TREATMENT_SUMMARY             	No treatment was involved in this study.
#SAMPLEPREP
SP:SAMPLEPREP_SUMMARY            	Samples were prepared by six seperate protocols. Among them, the first one was
SP:SAMPLEPREP_SUMMARY            	for amine acids and nucleosides, the second one was for bile acids and fatty
SP:SAMPLEPREP_SUMMARY            	acids, the third was for organic acids and carbohydrates by chemical
SP:SAMPLEPREP_SUMMARY            	derivatization, the fourth was for bacteria-derived metabolites, the fifth for
SP:SAMPLEPREP_SUMMARY            	acyl carnitines & lysophospholipids and the last was for other lipids. Details
SP:SAMPLEPREP_SUMMARY            	are shown in the uploaded file.
SP:SAMPLEPREP_PROTOCOL_FILENAME  	Preparation_protocols_for_M1-M6.pdf
SP:PROCESSING_STORAGE_CONDITIONS 	Room temperature
#CHROMATOGRAPHY
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	Amine acids and nucleosides were separated on an Acquity UPLC BEH Amide column
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	(1.7 Î¼m, 2.1mm Ã 100 mm, Waters). The mobile phase was consisting of
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	acetonitrile-water solution (9:1) as phase A, and acetonitrile-water solution
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	(5:5) as phase B with 10 mM ammonium formate and 1% formic acid as additives in
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	both phases. The binary gradient condition was set as follows: 0-0.5 min at 0%
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	B, 0.5-12.0 min from 0 to 40%, 12.0-15.0 min from 40% to 70%, 15.0-16.0 min at
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	70%, 16.0-17.0 min from 70% to 0%B with additional 3 min for re-equilibration.
CH:CHROMATOGRAPHY_SUMMARY        	The column was kept at 50 Â°C and the total flow rate was set at 0.3 mL/min.
CH:METHODS_ID                    	M1
CH:METHODS_FILENAME              	LC_parameters_targeted_metabolomic_analysis.pdf
CH:INSTRUMENT_NAME               	Shimadzu 20AD
CH:COLUMN_NAME                   	Waters ACQUITY UPLC BEH Amide (100 x 2.1mm,1.7um)
CH:COLUMN_TEMPERATURE            	50
CH:FLOW_GRADIENT                 	0-0.5 min at 0% B, 0.5-12.0 min from 0 to 40%B, 12.0-15.0 min from 40% to 70%B,
CH:FLOW_GRADIENT                 	15.0-16.0 min at 70%B, 16.0-17.0 min from 70% to 0%B with additional 3 min for
CH:FLOW_GRADIENT                 	re-equilibration.
